# LIBRARY — consolidated network function + code library

One browsable library of **all 2694 functions**: each entry has its reconstructed record
(behavior, I/O, side-effects, branches, corrected pseudocode, evidence) **and** its inline raw decompile.
Organized into subsystem volumes. For machine queries use `../indexes/functions.json` or `re.js search`.

## Volumes
- **[chat](chat.md)** — 33 functions (113 KB)
- **[errors](errors.md)** — 13 functions (42 KB)
- **[helpers](helpers.md)** — 288 functions (635 KB)
- **[initialization](initialization.md)** — 50 functions (118 KB)
- **[lobby](lobby.md)** — 71 functions (216 KB)
- **[menus](menus.md)** — 341 functions (1076 KB)
- **[packets](packets.md)** — 94 functions (205 KB)
- **[rooms](rooms.md)** — 322 functions (1006 KB)
- **[roster](roster.md)** — 192 functions (601 KB)
- **[session](session.md)** — 280 functions (805 KB)
- **[transport](transport.md)** — 307 functions (733 KB)
- **[ui](ui.md)** — 526 functions (1561 KB)
- **[unknown](unknown.md)** — 76 functions (204 KB)
- **[unrelated](unrelated.md)** — 101 functions (184 KB)
